                                                                              更改財政年度結算日


On January 17, 2024, the Board announced that it has resolved        於2024年1月17日，董事會公佈其已議決將本公司
to change the financial year end date of the Company from            的財政年度結算日由6月30日更改為12月31日，即
June 30 to December 31 with immediate effect. Accordingly, the       時生效。因此，隨附的當前財務報告期的綜合財務
accompanying consolidated financial statements for the current       報表涵蓋2023年7月1日起至2023年12月31日止六
financial period cover a period of six months from July 1, 2023 to   個月期間。然而，部分比較數字乃截至2023年6月
December 31, 2023. Certain comparative figures, however, are         30日止年度，因此可能不可直接比較。
for the year ended June 30, 2023, and hence may not be directly
comparable.

Non-IFRS Financial Measures                                                非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標
In evaluating the business, MINISO considers and uses adjusted             於評估業務時，名創優品考慮並使用經調整淨利潤
net profit and adjusted basic and diluted net earnings per share           和經調整每股基本及攤薄淨盈利作為審查及評估其
as supplemental measures to review and assess its operating                經營業績的補充指標。呈列此等非《國際財務報告準
performance. The presentation of these non-IFRS financial                  則》財務指標並非旨在視為獨立於或替代根據《國際
measures is not intended to be considered in isolation or as a             財務報告準則》編製及呈列的財務資料。名創優品
substitute for the financial information prepared and presented            將經調整淨利潤定義為不包括以權益結算的股份支
in accordance with IFRS. MINISO defines adjusted net profit as             付開支的期內利潤。名創優品以本公司權益股東應
profit for the period excluding equity-settled share-based payment         佔經調整淨利潤除以按《國際財務報告準則》基準計
expenses. MINISO computes adjusted basic and diluted net                   算每股基本及攤薄盈利所用的普通股數量計算經調
earnings per share by dividing adjusted net profit attributable to         整每股基本及攤薄淨盈利。
the equity shareholders of the Company by the number of ordinary
shares used in the basic and diluted earnings per share calculation
on an IFRS basis.

MINISO presents these non-IFRS financial measures because they             名創優品呈報此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指
are used by the management to evaluate its operating performance           標，因管理層以此等指標評估其經營業績及制定業
and formulate business plans. These non-IFRS financial measures            務計劃。此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標使其
enable the management to assess its operating results without              管理層可在不考慮名創優品認為並非未來營運表現
considering the impacts of the aforementioned non-cash and other           指標的非現金及其他調整項目影響的情況下評估其
adjustment items that MINISO does not consider to be indicative            經營業績。因此，名創優品認為使用此等非《國際財
of its operating performance in the future. Accordingly, MINISO            務報告準則》財務指標為投資者及其他人士以與管
believes that the use of these non-IFRS financial measures provides        理層及董事會相同的方式理解及評估其經營業績提
useful information to investors and others in understanding                供了有用的資料。
and evaluating its operating results in the same manner as the
management and the Board.

These non-IFRS financial measures are not defined under IFRS              此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標未於《國際財
and are not presented in accordance with IFRS. These non-IFRS             務報告準則》中有所界定，亦未根據《國際財務報告
financial measures have limitations as analytical tools. One of the       準則》呈列。此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標
key limitations of using these non-IFRS financial measures is that        作為分析工具存在局限性。使用此等非《國際財務報
they do not reflect all items of income and expense that affect           告準則》指標的主要限制之一為其不能反映影響名
MINISO’s operations. Further, these non-IFRS financial measures           創優品營運的所有收入及開支項目。此外，此等非
may differ from the non-IFRS information used by other companies,         《國際財務報告準則》財務指標可能與其他公司（包
including peer companies, and therefore their comparability may be        含同行公司）使用的非《國際財務報告準則》財務指
limited.                                                                  標不同，因此其可比較性可能有限。

BUSINESS REVIEW FOR THE SIX MONTHS                                       截至2023年12月31日止六個月的業
ENDED DECEMBER 31, 2023                                                  務回顧
We are a global value retailer offering a variety of trendy lifestyle    我們是一家提供以IP設計為特色的豐富多樣的潮流
products featuring IP design. Since we opened our first store            生活家居產品的全球價值零售商。自2013 年在中
in China in 2013, we have successfully incubated two brands              國開設第一家門店以來，我們已成功孵化了兩個品
– “MINISO” and “TOP TOY”. We have built our flagship brand               牌，分別是「名創優品」和「TOP TOY」。我們已將旗
“MINISO” as a globally recognized retail brand and established           艦品牌「名創優品」打造為全球認可的零售品牌，並
a store network worldwide. Our flagship brand “MINISO” offers            在全球範圍內建立了門店網絡。我們的旗艦品牌「名
a frequently-refreshed assortment of lifestyle products covering         創優品」提供種類繁多及高頻上新的生活家居產品，
diverse consumer needs, and consumers are attracted to our               可滿足消費者多樣化的需求，以時尚、創意、高品
products’ trendiness, creativeness, high quality and affordability.      質及高性價比的產品吸引消費者。

During the six months ended December 31, 2023, the total number          截至2023年12月31日止六個月期間，本集團於中
of MINISO stores in China and overseas markets increased from            國和海外市場的名創優品門店總數由截至2023年6
5,791 as of June 30, 2023 to 6,413 as of December 31, 2023. The          月30日的5,791家增加至截至2023年12月31日的
number of TOP TOY stores increased from 118 as of June 30,               6,413家，TOP TOY門店數量由截至2023年6月30
2023 to 148 as of December 31, 2023. For the six months ended            日的118家增加至截至2023年12月31日的148家。
December 31, 2023, the aggregate GMV of the Group reached                截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日止六個月，本集團產生的
approximately RMB14.3 billion.                                           GMV總計約人民幣143億元。

BUSINESS OUTLOOK                                                        業務展望
In spite of the uncertainties brought by the macro environment,         展望2024年，面對宏觀環境帶來的不確定性，我們
looking forward to 2024, we will remain focused on our long-term        仍將專注於我們的長期戰略目標，堅定地推進全球
strategic goals: delivering on our globalization strategy, bolstering   化、加強我們的產品實力並進一步優化門店網絡。
the strength of our product offerings and optimizing our store          未來，我們希望通過以下戰略以進一步實現業務增
network. Going forward, we expect to further grow our business by       長。
pursuing the following strategies.

Engaged in global competition, we will take cost advantages             我們將從成本領先和產品差異化兩個維度參與全球
and product differentiation as key points. While sticking to our        競爭，在始終堅持性價比基本盤不動搖的同時，以
value-for-money proposition, we will continue to produce high-          IP設計為特色，持續產出優質產品，讓生活好物更
quality products featuring IP design to make lifestyle products more    加時尚潮流。
fashionable and trendy.

At the same time, we will actively implement the “Super Store”          同時，我們亦會積極嘗試「超級門店」戰略，通過
strategy to build a strong brand image in consumers’ minds. We          「超級門店」在消費者心中建立強大的品牌形象，鎖
are also targeting “Big Beauty”, “Big Toys” and “Big IPs” as super      定「大美妝」、「大玩具」和「大IP」以打造超級品類，
categories, and further exploring the room for improving average        進一步探索平均單店GMV提升的空間。
GMV per store.

In China, we will expand and upgrade our store network by               在中國，我們將通過把握低線城市機遇，吸納更多
capitalizing on the opportunities in lower-tier cities and taking on    新名創合夥人，拓展並升級我們的門店網絡，進一
more new MINISO Retail Partners to further penetrate into the cities    步滲透我們已覆蓋的各大城市。
that we have already covered.

For overseas markets, we will further expand our store network          就海外市場而言，我們會因地制宜，於每個市場採
by adopting a flexible operating model for each market, and will        取靈活的運營模式，進一步拓展門店網絡，並持續
continue to expand our presence in strategic markets such as            深耕北美、亞洲及歐洲等戰略市場。
North America, Asia and Europe.

Gross Profit and Gross Margin                                            毛利及毛利率

the six months ended December 31, 2022 to RMB3,241.0 million             民幣1,985.7百萬元增加63.2%至截至2023年12月
for the six months ended December 31, 2023, and gross margin             31日止六個月的人民幣3,241.0百萬元，毛利率由

Foreign Exchange Risk                                                    匯兌風險
Our financial reporting currency is RMB and changes in foreign           我們的財務報告貨幣為人民幣，匯率變動會顯著影
exchange rates can significantly affect our reported results and         響我們報告的業績及綜合趨勢。此外，我們的經營
consolidated trends. In addition, our results of operations, including   業績（包括利潤率）受到匯率波動的影響。我們的國
profit margins, are affected by the fluctuation in foreign exchange      際業務主要以美元產生收入。一般而言，人民幣兌
rates. Our international operations generate revenues primarily in       美元貶值對我們的經營業績產生積極影響，而人民
U.S. dollars. Generally, a weakening of RMB against U.S. dollar has      幣兌美元升值則相反。我們並未使用任何衍生金融
a positive effect on our results of operations, while a strengthening    工具對沖有關風險。若我們需要將美元兌換成人民
of RMB against U.S. dollar has the opposite effect. We have not          幣用於營運，人民幣兌美元升值將對我們從兌換中
used any derivative financial instruments to hedge exposure to such      獲得的人民幣金額產生不利影響。相反，若我們決
risk. To the extent that we need to convert U.S. dollars into RMB for    定將人民幣兌換為美元用於支付股份或美國存託股
our operations, appreciation of RMB against U.S. dollar would have       股息或其他業務目的，則美元兌人民幣升值將對我
an adverse effect on RMB amount we receive from the conversion.          們獲得的美元金額產生不利影響。
Conversely, if we decide to convert RMB into U.S. dollars for the
purpose of making payments for dividends on our Shares or ADSs
or for other business purposes, appreciation of U.S. dollar against
RMB would have a negative effect on U.S. dollar amounts available
to us.

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                            主席及首席執行官
Code provision C.2.1 of the Corporate Governance Code                           《企業管治守則》的守則條文第C.2.1條建議（但並無
recommends, but does not require, that the roles of chairman of the             規定）董事會主席與首席執行官的角色應有區分且不
Board and chief executive officer should be separate and should                 應由同一人兼任。
not be performed by the same individual.

The Company deviates from this code provision as we do not                      本公司偏離該守則條文，因為我們並無單獨的主席
have a separate chairman and chief executive officer and Mr. Ye                 與首席執行官，葉先生現兼任本公司這兩個職務。
currently performs these two roles of the Company. Mr. Ye is our                葉先生為我們的創始人，深諳我們的業務營運及管
founder and has extensive experience in our business operations                 理。董事會認為，主席與首席執行官由同一人士兼
and management. The Board believes that vesting the roles of                    任可確保本集團內部的一致領導，並使本集團的整
both chairperson and chief executive officer in the same person                 體戰略規劃更為有效及高效。董事會認為現時的安
has the benefit of ensuring consistent leadership within our Group              排並不會損害權力及權責的平衡，而此結構將確保
and enables more effective and efficient overall strategic planning             本公司迅速及有效地作出及執行決定。董事會將在
for our Group. The Board considers that the balance of power and                考慮本集團的整體情況後，適時繼續審議及考慮區
authority for the present arrangement will not be impaired and                  分董事會主席與本公司首席執行官的職責。
this structure will enable our Company to make and implement
decisions promptly and effectively. The Board will continue to
review and consider splitting the roles of chairman of the Board
and the chief executive officer of our Company if and when it is
appropriate taking into account the circumstances of the Group as
a whole.

next or a subsequent annual general meeting or upon any specified          事均合資格於股東大會上膺選連任或由董事會重新
event or after any specified period in a written agreement between         委任。
the Company and the Director, if any; but no such term shall be
implied in the absence of express provision. Each Director whose
term of office expired shall be eligible for re-election at a meeting of
the Shareholders or re-appointment by the Board.

BOARD MEETINGS                                                          董事會會議
The Company adopts the practice of holding Board meetings               本公司採納定期舉行董事會會議的慣例，每年至少
regularly, at least four times a year, and at approximately quarterly   舉行四次會議，大約每季舉行一次。本公司就所有
intervals. Notices of not less than 14 days are given for all regular   定期董事會會議發出不少於14日的通知，以向全體
Board meetings to provide all Directors with an opportunity to          董事提供機會出席定期會議並將有關事項納入議程。
attend and include matters in the agenda for a regular meeting.

For other Board meetings and Board Committee meetings,                  就其他董事會會議及董事委員會會議而言，本公司
reasonable notice is generally given by the Company. The agenda         一般會發出合理通知。議程及隨附會議文件於董事
and accompanying meeting papers are provided to the Directors           會會議或董事委員會會議舉行前至少三天提供予董
or Board Committee members at least three days before the Board         事或董事委員會成員，以確保董事有充足時間審閱
meetings or Board Committee meetings to ensure that the Directors       有關文件及充分準備出席董事會會議或董事委員會
have sufficient time to review the papers and be adequately             會議。倘董事或董事委員會成員未能出席會議，則
prepared for the Board meetings or Board Committee meetings.            彼等會獲悉將予討論的事宜，並有機會於會議召開
When Directors or Board Committee members are unable to attend          前知會主席有關彼等的意見。會議紀錄由聯席公司
a meeting, they will be advised of the matters to be discussed and      秘書保存，副本將於全體董事間傳閱，以供參考及

prior to the meeting. Minutes of meetings are kept by the joint

Minutes of the Board meetings and Board Committee meetings are          董事會會議及董事委員會會議的會議記錄會充足詳

for comments within a reasonable time after the date on which the       意見。董事會會議記錄可供全體董事查閱。
meeting is held. The minutes of the Board meetings are open for

THE BOARD
The Board is the primary decision-making body of the Company             董事會為本公司的主要決策機構，負責監督本集團
and is responsible for overseeing the Group’s businesses,                的業務、戰略決策及表現，並透過指導及監督本公
strategic decisions and performance and is collectively responsible      司事務，共同負責促進本公司的成功。董事會客觀
for promoting the success of the Company by directing and                作出符合本公司利益的決策。全體董事（包括獨立非
supervising its affairs. The Board makes decisions objectively in        執行董事）為董事會帶來廣泛而寶貴的業務經驗、知
the interests of the Company. All Directors, including independent       識及專業精神，使其高效及有效地運作。
non-executive Directors, have brought a wide spectrum of valuable
business experience, knowledge and professionalism to the Board
for its efficient and effective functioning.

regularly reviews the functions and responsibilities delegated. The      級管理層於訂立任何重大交易前須取得董事會批准。
senior management needs to obtain approval from the Board prior
to entering into any significant transactions.

affairs of the Company, including: to approve and oversee all policy     利，包括：批准及監督所有政策及事宜、整體戰略
and matters, overall strategy and budgets, internal control and risk     及預算、內部監控及風險管理系統、重大交易（尤其

may involve conflict of interests), financial information, appointment   任及其他主要財務及營運事宜。
of Directors and other major financial and operational issues.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE FUNCTION                                          企業管治職能

      development of the Directors and senior management of the              持續專業發展；
      Company;

      compliance manual applicable to employees and the                      及合規手冊；
      Directors;

      on corporate governance, and to make recommendations and               向董事會提出建議及匯報相關事宜；
      report related issues to the Board;

2020 SHARE INCENTIVE PLAN                                              2020年股份激勵計劃
The 2020 Share Incentive Plan was first adopted by the Board           2020年股份激勵計劃於2020年9月首次獲董事會採
in September 2020 and was further amended in June 2022, the            納，並於2022年6月進一步修訂，修訂於上市後生
amendments of which took effect upon the Listing. Since the            效。自上市起，2020年股份激勵計劃受《上市規則》
Listing, the 2020 Share Incentive Plan was subject to Chapter          第17章規限。本公司倚賴《上市規則》新第17章（自
17 of the Listing Rules. The Company relies on the transitional        2023年1月1日起生效）所規定的過渡安排。
arrangements provided for under the new Chapter 17 of the Listing
Rules which became effective on January 1, 2023.

are as described below.                                                下。

Purpose                                                                目的
The purposes of the 2020 Share Incentive Plan are to attract           2020年股份激勵計劃旨在吸引及挽留合資格人員，
and retain qualified personnel, provide incentives to employees,       激勵本集團僱員、顧問及董事，促進我們的業務取
consultants and directors of our Group, and promote the success        得成功。
of our business.

Eligible Participants                                                  合資格參與者
Eligible participants include employees, consultants, and directors    合資格參與者包括本集團僱員、顧問及董事（「參與
of our Group (the “Participants”). The plan administrator may,         者」）。計劃管理人可不時從將獲授以購股權（「購股
from time to time, select from among all Participants to whom          權」）、購買受限制股份（「受限制股份」）的權利或購
awards in the form of share options (the “Options”), a right to        買受限制股份單位的權利（統稱「獎勵」）的所有參與
purchase restricted Shares (the “Restricted Shares”) or a right to     者中進行選擇，並將釐定各購股權的性質及金額。
purchase RSUs (collectively, the “Awards”), will be granted and will
determine the nature and amount of each option.

 evaluating the significant assumptions used by the Group             成本時所使用的市場租金，方式是將我們利用外部數據
 in estimating the recoverable amount. Specifically, the              獨立確定的市場租金範圍與貴集團的估計值進行比較。
 forecasted sales used to estimate the self-operated stores’
 value in use were challenging to test as they represented
 subjective determinations of future sales trends that
 were also sensitive to variation. Minor changes to those
 assumptions could have had a significant effect on the
 Group’s assessment of the forecasted sales. Furthermore,
 specialized skills and knowledge were needed to assess
 the market rentals used to estimate the self-operated
 stores’ fair value less costs of disposal.
 我們將與若干直營店相關的物業、廠房及設備及使用權資產
 的減值評估列為一項關鍵審計事項。在評估 貴集團用於預
 估可回收金額的重大假設時，核數師須作出較高程度的判
 斷。具體而言，對用於估計直營店使用價值的預測銷售額的
 測試具有挑戰性，因為它們代表了對未來銷售趨勢的主觀判
 斷，對變化也很敏感。該等假設的微小變動能夠對 貴集團
 的預測銷售額評估產生重大影響。此外，專業技能及知識在
 評估市場租金時不可或缺，而市場租金用於估算直營店的公
 允價值減出售成本。

        1.1 General information                                              1.1 一般資料
               MINISO Group Holding Limited (the “Company”) was                名創優品集團控股有限公司（「本公司」）
               incorporated in the Cayman Islands on January 7,                於2020 年1 月7 日根據開曼群島法例第
               2020, as an exempted company with limited liability             22章《公司法》  （1961年第3號法例，經
               under the Companies Law, Cap.22 (Law 3 of 1961, as              合併及修訂）於開曼群島註冊成立為獲
               consolidated and revised) of the Cayman Islands. The            豁免有限責任公司。本公司於2020年10
               Company completed its initial public offering (“IPO”)           月15日完成首次公開發售，且本公司的
               on October 15, 2020 and the Company’s American                  美國存託股自此已在紐約證券交易所上
               Depositary Shares (“ADSs”) have been listed on the              市。本公司的每一份美國存託股代表四
               New York Stock Exchange since then. Each ADS of                 股普通股。本公司普通股亦於2022年7
               the Company represents four ordinary shares. The                月13日在香港聯合交易所有限公司（「香
               Company’s ordinary shares have also been listed on              港聯交所」）上市。
               The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Hong
               Kong Stock Exchange”) since July 13, 2022.

               The accompanying consolidated financial statements              隨附綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬
               comprise the Company and its subsidiaries (together,            公司（統稱「本集團」）。本集團主要在
               the “Group”). The Group is principally engaged in the           中華人民共和國（「中國」）及亞洲其他國
               retail and wholesale of lifestyle and pop toy products          家、美洲和歐洲等地從事生活家居產品

               Amendments to IAS 12, Deferred Tax related                           《國際會計準則》第12號（修訂本），與
               to Assets and Liabilities arising from a Single                       單一交易產生的資產及負債相關的遞延
               Transaction                                                           稅項
               The amendments narrow the scope of the initial                        該等修訂縮小初始確認豁免的範圍，使
               recognition exemption such that it does not apply                     其不適用於在初始確認時產生等額及抵
               to transactions that give rise to equal and offsetting                銷暫時性差異的交易，如租賃及退役負
               temporary differences on initial recognition such as                  債。就租賃及退役負債而言，相關遞延
               leases and decommissioning liabilities. For leases and                稅項資產及負債須於所呈列的最早比較
               decommissioning liabilities, the associated deferred                  期間開始時確認，而任何累計影響則確
               tax assets and liabilities are required to be recognized              認為於該日對留存收益或其他權益組成
               from the beginning of the earliest comparative period                 部分的調整。就所有其他交易而言，該
               presented, with any cumulative effect recognized as an                等修訂適用於最早呈列期間開始後發生
               adjustment to retained earnings or other components                   的交易。
               of equity at that date. For all other transactions,
               the amendments are applied to those transactions
               that occur after the beginning of the earliest period
               presented.

               Prior to the amendments, the Group did not apply the                 於修訂前，本集團並未就租賃交易應用
               initial recognition exemption to lease transactions and              初始確認豁免，並已確認相關遞延稅
               had recognized the related deferred tax, except that the             項，惟本集團先前於某一日期釐定使用
               Group previously determined the temporary difference                 權資產及以淨額為基準，按單項交易產
               arising from a right-of-use asset and the related lease              生的相關租賃負債的暫時性差異除外。
               liability on a net basis on the basis they arise from a              於該等修訂後，本集團已分別釐定有關
               single transaction. Following the amendments, the                    使用權資產及租賃負債的暫時性差異。
               Group has determined the temporary differences in                    該變動主要影響附註10(c)中遞延稅項資
               relation to right-of-use assets and lease liabilities                產及負債組成部分的披露，但並不影響
               separately. The change primarily impacts disclosures                 綜合財務狀況表中呈列的整體遞延稅項
               of components of deferred tax assets and liabilities in              結餘，因為相關遞延稅項結餘符合《國際
               Note 10(c), but does not impact the overall deferred tax             會計準則》第12號項下的抵銷條件。
               balances presented in the consolidated statement of
               financial position as the related deferred tax balances
               qualify for offsetting under IAS 12.

2   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                          2      重大會計政策（續）
    (continued)
    (d)   Basis of consolidation                                                 (d)    綜合基準
          (i)   Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                              (i)    附屬公司及非控股權益
                Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group.                             附屬公司為受本集團控制的實體。
                The Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or                            如果本集團因其參與某一實體事務
                has rights, to variable returns from its involvement                           而可獲取或有權收取可變回報並可
                with the entity and has the ability to affect those                            通過其對該實體擁有的權力影響該
                returns through its power over the entity. When                                等回報，則本集團控制該實體。在
                assessing whether the Group has power, only                                    評估本集團是否擁有權力時，只考
                substantive rights (held by the Group and other                                慮實質性權利（由本集團和其他各
                parties) are considered.                                                       方持有）。


                The financial statements of subsidiaries are                                   附屬公司的財務報表自控制權開始
                included in the consolidated financial statements                              之日起至控制權終止之日均包含於
                from the date on which control commences                                       綜合財務報表中。集團內部餘額、
                until the date on which control ceases. Intra-                                 交易及現金流，以及集團內部交易
                group balances, transactions and cash flows and                                所產生的任何未變現利潤，在編製
                any unrealized profits arising from intra-group                                綜合財務報表時均全數抵銷。集團
                transactions are eliminated in full in preparing                               內交易產生的未變現虧損僅在並無
                the consolidated financial statements. Unrealized                              出現減值跡象的情況下以與未變現
                losses resulting from intra-group transactions are                             收益相同的方法予以抵銷。
                eliminated in the same way as unrealized gains
                but only to the extent that there is no evidence of
                impairment.

                Non-controlling interests represent the equity in a                            非控股權益指非直接或間接歸屬於
                subsidiary not attributable directly or indirectly to                          本公司的附屬公司權益，且本集團

                has not agreed any additional terms with the                                   何額外條款，致使本集團整體須就
                holders of those interests which would result in the                           該等權益承擔符合金融負債定義的
                Group as a whole having a contractual obligation in                            合同責任。非控股權益按其於收購
                respect of those interests that meets the definition                           日在附屬公司可辨認淨資產所佔的
                of a financial liability. Non-controlling interests are                        比例進行初始計量。
                measured initially at their proportionate share of
                the subsidiary’s net identifiable assets at the date
                of acquisition.

    (g)   Leased assets                                                        (g)    租賃資產
          At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                      在合約開始時，本集團會評估該合約是
          the contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or                    否屬於或包含租賃。倘一份合約轉移於
          contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to                      一段時間內使用已確認資產的控制權以
          control the use of an identified asset for a period of                      換取對價，則該合約屬於或包含租賃。
          time in exchange for consideration. Control is conveyed                     如客戶既有權指示已確定資產的使用，
          where the customer has both the right to direct the use                     又有權從該使用中獲得絕大部分經濟利
          of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all of                  益，則控制權被轉移。
          the economic benefits from that use.

          As a lessee                                                                 作為承租人
          Where the contract contains lease component(s) and                          當合約包含租賃組成部分和非租賃組成
          non-lease component(s), the Group has elected not                           部分時，本集團選擇不將非租賃組成部
          to separate non-lease components and accounts for                           分分開，並將每個租賃組成部分和任何
          each lease component and any associated non-lease                           關聯的非租賃組成部分作為所有租賃的
          components as a single lease component for all leases.                      單一租賃組成部分進行列賬。

                      discounting is material:

                             determined at initial recognition or an                             確定的實際利率或其近似值。
                             approximation thereof.

                      The maximum period considered when estimating                          估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮的最長
                      ECLs is the maximum contractual period over                            期限為本集團面臨信貸風險的最長
                      which the Group is exposed to credit risk.                             合約期。


                      In measuring ECLs, the Group takes into account                        在計量預期信貸虧損時，本集團考
                      reasonable and supportable information that                            慮合理及有理據而無需付出過多的

                      conditions.

                             the issuer.

                     Write-off policy                                                            撇銷政策
                     The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is                           若無實際收回可能，則（部分或全
                     written off (either partially or in full) to the extent                     部）撇銷該金融資產的賬面總值。
                     that there is no realistic prospect of recovery. This                       這種情況通常發生在本集團確定債
                     is generally the case when the Group determines                             務人沒有資產或收入來源可產生足
                     that the debtor does not have assets or sources of                          夠的現金流以償還將被撇銷的金
                     income that could generate sufficient cash flows                            額。
                     to repay the amounts subject to the write-off.

                      Subsequent recoveries of an asset that was                                 過往撇銷資產的後續回收在回收期
                      previously written off are recognized as a reversal                        間於損益確認為減值撥回。
                      of impairment in profit or loss in the period in
                      which the recovery occurs.

        an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                 計估計的修訂是預先確定的。

               30, 2023, the sales of most of the Group’s overseas                     防控措施，本集團海外直營店及代理門

              online payment platforms. Withdrawal of the balances retained              的餘額可根據本集團指示隨時提取。該等金

27 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND                                     27 財務風險管理及公允價值
   FAIR VALUES
  Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency                 本集團在日常業務過程中承受信貸、流動性、
  risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s business.                利率及貨幣風險。下文呈列本集團所承受的上
  The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk                述風險及本集團為管理該等風險所採用的財務
  management policies and practices used by the Group to                    風險管理政策及實務。
  manage these risks are described below.

  (a)   Credit risk                                                         (a)    信貸風險
        Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will                    信貸風險指交易對手未履行其合約責任
        default on its contractual obligations resulting in a                      而導致本集團產生財務虧損的風險。本
        financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is                    集團的信貸風險主要歸因於貿易及其他
        primarily attributable to trade and other receivables.                     應收款項。由於交易對手為本集團認為
        The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash                      信貸風險低且具有高信貸質量的銀行及
        and cash equivalents, restricted cash and term deposits                    金融機構，因此本集團承擔來自現金及
        is limited because the counterparties are banks and                        現金等價物、受限制現金及定期存款的
        financial institutions with high-credit-quality, for which                 信貸風險有限。
        the Group considers having low credit risk.

        Trade receivables                                                          貿易應收款項
        The Group’s trade receivables mainly derive from                           本集團的貿易應收款項主要來自於向代
        sales of goods to distributors and franchisees. The                        理商及加盟商銷售貨物。本集團面臨的
        Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by                    信貸風險主要受每個客戶的個別特徵（而
        the individual characteristics of each customer rather                     非客戶經營所在的行業或國家）影響，
        than the industry or country in which the customers                        因此，當本集團與個別客戶有大額往來
        operate and therefore significant concentrations of                        時，即面對信貸風險高度集中的情況。
        credit risk primarily arise when the Group has significant                 於 2023 年 6 月 30 日及 2023 年 12 月 31
        exposure to individual customers. At June 30, 2023 and                     日，貿易應收款項總額的40%及40%分
        December 31, 2023, 40% and 40% of the total trade                          別來自本集團的五大債務人。
        receivables were due from the Group’s five largest
        debtors, respectively.

        Individual credit evaluations are performed on all                         我們會對所有要求獲得超出若干金額的
        customers requiring credit over a certain amount. These                    信貸的客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等評
        evaluations focus on the customer’s history of making                      估針對客戶到期還款記錄及現時還款能
        payments when due and current ability to pay and take                      力，並考慮客戶的具體信息以及與客戶
        into account information specific to the customer as well                  經營所在經濟環境有關的信息。
        as pertaining to the economic environment in which the
        customer operates.

        (c)    Interest rate risk                                                             (c)   利率風險
               Interest rate risk is the risk that the fair value or future                         利率風險是指金融工具的公允價值或未
               cash flows of a financial instrument will fluctuate                                  來現金流量因市場利率變動而波動的風
               because of changes in market interest rates. The Group                               險。於各報告期末，本集團並無將任何
               does not account for any fixed-rate financial instruments                            固定利率金融工具按公允價值計入損
               at fair value through profit or loss at the end of each                              益。因此，按固定利率計息的金融工具
               reporting periods. Therefore, interest-bearing financial                             不會使本集團面臨公允價值利率風險。
               instruments at fixed rates do not expose the Group to                                本集團的利率風險主要來自受限制現金
               fair value interest rate risk. The Group’s interest rate risk                        及以浮動利率計息的銀行存款，這使本
               arises primarily from restricted cash and cash at bank                               集團面臨現金流量利率風險。本集團根
               at variable rates, which exposes the Group to cash flow                              據目前的市場情況釐定固定利率和浮動
               interest rate risk. The Group determines the appropriate                             利率計息工具的適當權重，並定期進行
               weightings of the fixed and floating rate interest-bearing                           審查和監測，以實現固定利率和浮動利
               instruments based on the current market conditions and                               率風險的適當組合。本集團並無訂立金
               performs regular reviews and monitoring to achieve an                                融衍生工具對沖其利率風險。
               appropriate mix of fixed and floating rate exposure. The
               Group does not enter into financial derivatives to hedge
               interest rate risk.

                     unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs                                       非使用重大不可觀察輸入數
                     are inputs for which market data are not                                       據計量的公允價值。不可觀
                     available.                                                                     察輸入數據為無市場數據的
                                                                                                    輸入數據。

               •     Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using                           •      第三層級估值：使用重大不

33 DIRECTORS’ EMOLUMENTS                                             33 董事酬金
  Directors’ emoluments disclosed pursuant to the Rules                   根據《香港聯交所證券上市規則》、香港《公司

                                       considers to have a similar level of development potential

                                       為具有相似水平發展潛力的其他城市
